Implement soft_logsumexp stable:
```
def softmax(u):
    v = u - u.max()
    e=np.exp(v)
    return e/e.sum()
```

Let's design code in detail:

Helper functions:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
import scipy as sp
import scipy.optimize, scipy.fft, scipy.sparse

def exact_eval_np(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<0.01: return inf
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    return float(2*a.size*np.max(c)/S*S)
```
But final exact via evaluate_sequence; internal can use `score_np` with normalization to sum S (not necessarily 1). Use scale invariant.

FFT convolution:
```
def convolve_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    N=1
    while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    fa2=fa*fa
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa2,N)[:2*n-1]
    # roundoff small negative; set min 0?
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c
```
For n<=2048 maybe direct `np.convolve` simpler and exact enough. But FFT has tiny negative; we take max, roundoff won't affect max likely. For final exact, use evaluator.

Score internal:
```
def score(a):
    S=a.sum()
    if S<0.01: return inf
    c=convolve_fft(a) if a.size>256 else np.convolve(a,a)
    return 2*a.size*np.max(c)/(S*S)
```
Normalize to S=1 for optimization.

Directional extrema? For gradient:
```
def grad_max_smooth(a, c, tau):
   m=c.max()
   z=(c-m)/tau
   e=np.exp(z); w=e/e.sum()
   # adjoint: v_l = sum_j a_j w_{l+j}
   if a.size <= 1024:
       v=np.convolve(w, a[::-1], mode='full')[n-1:3*n-2]
   else:
       N=next power of two >=3*n? We need v length n. Use FFT adjoint.
   g=2*v
   return g
```

Build A:
```
def build_A_rows(f, klist):
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    n=f.size
    for k in klist:
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
        if hi<lo: continue
        js=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
        rows.append(np.full(hi-lo+1,k))
        cols.append(js)
        data.append(f[k-js])
    A=sparse.csr_matrix((concat data, (concat rows, concat cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n)) # But rows only selected, A_ub need shape with selected row count? For linprog, A_ub should be (#selected, n), not full with zeros. Use rows 0..K-1 corresponding klist.
```

```
def top_subgradient_step(a, S, rng, T=256, tau=0.02, eta=None):
   c=conv(a) # length 2n-1
   top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -T)[-T:]
   vals=c[top_idx]
   # weights softmax relative to max(vals/tau)
   z=(vals - vals.max())/tau
   w = exp(z); w/=w.sum()
   # compute v = sum_k w_k * a shifted
   v=np.zeros(n)
   for k, wk in zip(top_idx, w):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if lo>hi: continue
       # v[lo:hi+1] += wk * a[k-lo : k-hi-1 : -1]
       # Need correct slice. k-lo is max index, k-hi is min index; we want descending from max to min inclusive.
       v[lo:hi+1] += wk * a[k-lo : k-hi-1 : -1]
   g=2*v
   # softmax gradient if using u? If we maintain u=log(a/S)
```

```
import time, math
import numpy as np
import scipy as sp
import scipy.optimize as so
import scipy.sparse as sps

def ensure_height():
    try: return np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
    except: return None

def conv_full(a):
    n=a.size
    if n <= 1024: return np.convolve(a,a)
    N=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(c,0.0,c)
    return c

def score_np(a): ...

def normalize_S(a,S): return a*S/a.sum()
```


Let's write `sota_loop`:
```
def sota_loop(f, N, rng, deadline, K_limit, full_constraints=False):
    S=math.sqrt(2*N)
    f=normalize(f,S)
    M=score_m(f)
    f_current=f.copy(); M_current=M
    f_best=f.copy(); M_best=M
    iter=0
    fail_count=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and iter < 50:
       c=conv_full(f_current)
       if full_constraints and N<=2500:
          klist=np.arange(2*N-1)
       else:
          klist=choose_constraints(c,K_limit,rng)
       A=build_A(f_current,klist)
       # LP
       b=None
       try:
          obj = 1.0 + 0.03*rng.random(N)
          U = S * (2.0 if N>2500 else 1.5)
          res = sopt.linprog(-obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=M_current*np.ones(len(klist)), bounds=[(0.0,U)]*N, method='highs', options={'time_limit': 40.0})
          if res.status==0: b=np.clip(res.x,0,None)
       except Exception: pass
       iter +=1
       if b is not None and b.sum()>0:
          g = b * (S / b.sum())
          best_local=f_current.copy(); best_local_M=M_current
          for t in (0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5):
             ft=(1-t)*f_current + t*g
             ft=normalize(ft,S)
             Mt=score_m(ft)
             if Mt < best_local_M:
                 best_local_M=Mt; best_local=ft.copy()
          if best_local_M < M_best:
             M_best=best_local_M; f_best=best_local.copy(); f_current=best_local.copy(); M_current=M_best; fail_count=0
          else:
             # maybe use best local if improves current even if not global
             if best_local_M < M_current:
                 M_current=best_local_M; f_current=best_local.copy()
             # else random perturbation to escape
             fail_count +=1
             if fail_count % 3==0:
                 f_current = best_local.copy() if best_local_M<M_current else f_current
                 f_current = add_smooth_noise(f_current, S, rng, 0.1)
                 M_current=score_m(f_current)
       else:
          fail_count +=1
          f_current = add_smooth_noise(f_current, S, rng, 0.1)
          M_current=score_m(f_current)
       # Also random block perturbations? maybe after every few
       # update outside? return best
       # Check time inside loops
    return f_best, M_best, f_current, M_current
```
Need `score_m(a)` assumes sum S? It returns max conv, which equals score if sum S. But if a not normalized, compute 2n max/S^2. We can always normalize in loop. Use general score.

   norm=...; if small break.

   if no improvement:
      # try random direction if stuck
      if iter % 50==0: u += 0.01*(np.linalg.norm(u)/n)*rng.normal(0,1,n); M_current=...
      shrink tau maybe tau*=0.8
   else M_current=...

def make_A_rows(f, n, klist):
    rows_parts=[]; cols_parts=[]; data_parts=[]
    for idx,k in enumerate(klist):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       length=hi-lo+1
       if length:
          cols_parts.append(np.arange(lo,hi+1))
          rows_parts.append(np.full(length, idx, dtype=np.int32))

          data_parts.append(f[indices])
    if parts empty... 
    data=np.concatenate(data_parts); rows=...; cols=...
    return sps.csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(len(klist),n))

def downsample(arr,m):
   if arr.size < m: upsample
   idx = np.linspace(0, arr.size-1, m)
   return np.interp(idx, np.arange(arr.size), arr)

```
def linprog_safe(c,A,bub,U,N):
   try:
      df = [(0.0, float(U))] * N
   except MemoryError: pass
   try:
      return sopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=bub, bounds=(0.0,float(U)), method='highs', options={'time_limit': 30})
   except Exception as ex1:
      try:
         return sopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=bub, bounds=(0.0,float(U)), method='highs')
      except Exception:
         df=[(0.0,float(U))]*N
         return sopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=bub, bounds=df, method='highs')
```

```
def choose_constraints(c,K,rng):
   total=c.size
   if K>=total: return np.arange(total)
   topK=min(200,K)
   top=np.argpartition(c, -topK)[-topK:]

   # add neighbors deterministic
   for kk in top:
      for d in (-2,-1,1,2):
         if 0<=kk+d<total: idx_set.add(kk+d)
   # if too many, keep top plus some neighbors

         if x not in top_set and x not in idx_set? Need avoid duplicates; use separate seen.
   else fill random
```

`_conv_full(a)`:
```
n=a.size
if n <= 2048:
   return np.convolve(a,a)
N=1
while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
c=c[:2*n-1]
c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0)
return c
```
For n small direct. For n large FFT length maybe 16384.

```
       except Exception:

           except Exception: res=None
```

`bounds=(0.0,U)` may fail with sparse? If so, we need list. Add helper:
```
def _linprog(c_obj,A,bub,U,n):
    opts={'time_limit':30.0}
    for bounds in ((0.0,U), None):
       try:
          if bounds is None:
             return sopt.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A,b_ub=bub, method='highs', options=opts)
          else:
             return sopt.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A,b_ub=bub,bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=opts)
       except Exception:
          pass
    # fallback list
    try:
       lb=np.zeros(n); ub=np.full(n,U)
       return sopt.linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A,b_ub=bub,bounds=(lb,ub),method='highs',options=opts)
    except Exception:
       return None
```

```
def _polish(f, deadline, max_iter=3000, top=192, rand=64):
   S=f.sum(); n=f.size; tau=0.1*max(_score(f),1e-6)? Actually c max ~M. tau = max(0.05, 0.1*M?) For M~2, tau=0.2. Let's set tau=0.1.
   tiny=1e-12
   a=np.clip(f,1e-12,None)
   u=np.log(a/S)
   best=a.copy(); M_best=_score(a); cur=a.copy(); M_cur=M_best
   rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
      a=_norm(np.exp(u - np.max(u))*S,S)
      c=_conv_full(a)
      total=c.size
      selT=min(top,total)
      topIdx=np.argpartition(c, -selT)[-selT:]
      rT=min(rand, max(1,total//100))
      rIdx=rng.integers(0,total,rT)
      idx=np.unique(np.concatenate((topIdx,rIdx)))
      vals=c[idx]
      z=(vals - vals.max())/tau
      w=np.exp(z); w/=w.sum()
      v=np.zeros(n)
      for kk,ww in zip(idx,w):
          lo=max(0,kk-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,kk)
          if lo<=hi:
              v[lo:hi+1] += ww * a[kk - np.arange(lo,hi+1)]
      g=2*v
      gp=g - np.mean(g)
      scale=np.linalg.norm(gp)
      if scale < 1e-12*scale? if scale < 1e-10: break
      lr=0.1/((scale/n)+1e-12)
      accepted=False
      for mu in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
          u_try=u - lr*mu*gp
          a_try=_norm(np.exp(u_try - np.max(u_try))*S,S)
          Mt=_score(a_try)
          if Mt < M_cur:
              cur=a_try.copy(); M_cur=Mt; u=u_try.copy(); accepted=True
              if Mt < M_best: best=cur.copy(); M_best=Mt
              break
      if not accepted:
          if it % 50 == 0:
              # random kick
              kick = rng.standard_normal(n)
              # smooth maybe
              u = u + 0.02*(np.linalg.norm(u)/n+1e-9)*kick
              cur=_norm(np.exp(u - np.max(u))*S,S)
              M_cur=_score(cur)
              if M_cur<M_best: best=cur.copy(); M_best=M_cur
          if tau > 0.01: tau *= 0.8
   return best,M_best
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(1.0,float(budget_s)-10.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=np.asarray([],float)
    try:
       p=height_sequence_1
       parent=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
    except Exception:
       parent=np.array([],float)
    # sanitize parent
    if parent.size and np.isfinite(parent).all():
       parent=np.clip(parent,0,1000)
    else: parent=np.array([],float)
    # choose large N
    if parent.size >= 256:
       N=parent.size
    else:
       N=2048
       parent=np.ones(N)
    best_arr=parent.copy()
    best_score=_score(best_arr)
    # ensure valid fallback
    fallback=np.ones(min(N,64)); fallback_score=_score(fallback)
    if fallback_score < best_score: update
    # if small budget, just quick blocks
    time_left=deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left > 200 and N >= 512:
        # coarse if N large
        n_coarse = N if N <= 2000 else 1024
        if n_coarse < 256: n_coarse=512
        coarse_deadline = time.monotonic() + min(240.0, time_left*0.3?)
        # Use full SOTA if n_coarse <= 1024 and time_left>200
        init_coarse = _downsample(parent,n_coarse) if parent.size!=n_coarse else parent
        c_best,c_score,c_cur,c_M = _sota(n_coarse, coarse_deadline, init=init_coarse, full=True, max_iter=40)
        if c_score < best_score: best_arr=c_best; best_score=c_score
        if N != n_coarse:

        else: init_large=c_best
        # large SOTA subset
        large_deadline = time.monotonic() + min(450.0, max(0, deadline - time.monotonic())*0.5?)
    else: init_large=parent
```

```
def _resample(arr, m):
   n=arr.size
   if n==m: return arr.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   return np.interp(x_new,x_old,arr)
```

```
def _call_linprog(c, A, bub, U, n):
    attempts = [
       dict(method='highs', options={'time_limit':30.0}, bounds=(0.0,U)),
       dict(method='highs', bounds=(0.0,U)),
       dict(method='highs', options={'time_limit':30.0}),
       dict(method='highs'),
    ]
    for at in attempts:
       try:
          bounds=at.get('bounds', None)
          if bounds is not None:
             kwargs={'bounds': bounds}
          else:
             try:
                lb=np.zeros(n); ub=np.full(n,U); kwargs={'bounds':(lb,ub)}
             except: kwargs={}
          return sopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=bub, **at=None?)
```

```
  try: return sopt.linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=bub,bounds=(0.0,U),method='highs',options=opts)
  except Exception: pass
for opts in [{'time_limit':30.0}, {}]:

    lb=np.zeros(n); ub=np.full(n,U)
    return sopt.linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=bub,bounds=(lb,ub),method='highs',options=opts)
  except Exception: pass
# final no bounds? but LP unbounded; skip
return None
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy import optimize as sopt
from scipy import sparse as sps


def _parent_array():
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1  # pre-imported by the harness
        a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        a = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    return a


def _conv_full(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 2048:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = 1 << max(1, (need - 1).bit_length())
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, nfft)[:need]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_full(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _normalize(a, total):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.full_like(a, total / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (total / s)


def _resample(a, m):
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(m)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n <= 1:
        return np.full(m, float(a.item()) if n else 1.0)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    return np.interp(y, x, a)


def _smooth_noise(f, rng, strength=0.2):
    n = int(f.size)
    s = float(f.sum())
    if n < 8 or s <= 0:
        return _normalize(f, s)
    w = max(3, n // 80 | 0)
    k = np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64) / float(w)
    noise = np.convolve(rng.standard_normal(n), k, mode="same")
    noise -= noise.mean()
    scale = float(np.linalg.norm(noise))
    if scale > 0:
        scale = strength / scale
    else:
        scale = 0.0
    g = np.clip(f * (1.0 + scale * noise), 0.0, None)
    return _normalize(g, s)


def _random_block(f, rng):
    n = int(f.size)
    g = f.copy()
    lo_w = max(1, n // 250)
    hi_w = max(lo_w + 1, n // 14)
    width = int(rng.integers(lo_w, hi_w))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
    g[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.18)))
    if rng.random() < 0.25:
        g = 0.5 * (g + g[::-1])
    return _normalize(g, float(f.sum()))


def _make_A_full(f, n):
    # A @ b = full_convolve(f, b), A_{k,j} = f_{k-j} when 0 <= k-j < n.
    # Offsets 0..n-1, each diagonal is f.
    offs = list(range(n))
    diags = [f] * n
    return sps.diags(diags, offs, shape=(2 * n - 1, n), format="csr")


def _make_A_rows(f, n, klist):
    rows_parts = []
    cols_parts = []
    data_parts = []
    klist = np.asarray(klist, dtype=np.int64)
    for r, k in enumerate(klist):
        lo = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, int(k))
        if lo > hi:
            continue
        js = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int32)
        idx = int(k) - js.astype(np.int64)
        rows_parts.append(np.full(js.size, r, dtype=np.int32))
        cols_parts.append(js)
        data_parts.append(f[idx])
    if not data_parts:
        return None
    rows = np.concatenate(rows_parts)
    cols = np.concatenate(cols_parts)
    data = np.concatenate(data_parts)
    return sps.csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(len(klist), n))


def _choose_constraints(c, K, rng):
    total = int(c.size)
    K = int(K)
    if K >= total:
        return np.arange(total, dtype=np.int64)
    topK = min(max(24, K // 5), total)
    top = np.argpartition(c, -topK)[-topK:].astype(np.int64)
    seen = set(int(x) for x in top)
    idx = [int(x) for x in top]
    for x in top:
        x = int(x)
        for d in (-2, -1, 1, 2):
            y = x + d
            if 0 <= y < total and y not in seen and len(idx) < K:
                idx.append(y)
                seen.add(y)
    while len(idx) < K:
        y = int(rng.integers(0, total))
        if y not in seen:
            idx.append(y)
            seen.add(y)
    return np.asarray(idx[:K], dtype=np.int64)


def _linprog_safe(c_obj, A, bub, upper, n):
    opts_list = [
        {"time_limit": 30.0},
        {},
    ]
    for opts in opts_list:
        try:
            res = sopt.linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=bub,
                bounds=(0.0, float(upper)),
                method="highs",
                options=opts,
            )
            return res
        except Exception:
            pass
    for opts in opts_list:
        try:
            lb = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            ub = np.full(n, float(upper), dtype=np.float64)
            res = sopt.linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=bub,
                bounds=(lb, ub),
                method="highs",
                options=opts,
            )
            return res
        except Exception:
            pass
    return None


def _sota_loop(n, deadline, init, full, K_limit, rng, max_iter):
    n = int(n)
    total = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    f = _normalize(np.asarray(init, dtype=np.float64), total)
    if np.var(f) < 1e-18 * float(np.mean(f) ** 2):
        f = _smooth_noise(f, rng, 0.25)

    cur = f.copy()
    mcur = _score(cur)
    best = f.copy()
    mbest = mcur
    fail = 0
    it = 0

    use_full = bool(full) and n <= 1200
    if not use_full:
        K_limit = int(K_limit)
        if n > 4000:
            K_limit = min(K_limit, 800)
        else:
            K_limit = min(K_limit, 1200)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iter:
        c = _conv_full(cur)
        if c.size == 0 or not math.isfinite(mcur):
            break

        if use_full:
            A = _make_A_full(cur, n)
            mrows = 2 * n - 1
        else:
            klist = _choose_constraints(c, K_limit, rng)
            A = _make_A_rows(cur, n, klist)
            mrows = len(klist)

        if A is None or mrows == 0:
            it += 1
            continue

        bub = mcur * np.ones(mrows, dtype=np.float64)
        obj = 1.0 + 0.02 * rng.random(n)
        c_obj = -obj
        upper = float(2.0 * total)

        res = _linprog_safe(c_obj, A, bub, upper, n)
        it += 1
        A = None

        b = None
        try:
            x = getattr(res, "x", None)
            if x is not None:
                x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
                if x.size == n:
                    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
                    if x.sum() > 0 and np.isfinite(x.sum()):
                        b = x
        except Exception:
            b = None

        improved_now = False
        if b is not None:
            g = b * (total / float(b.sum()))
            best_try = None
            best_try_m = float("inf")
            for t in (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5):
                ft = (1.0 - t) * cur + t * g
                ft = _normalize(ft, total)
                mt = _score(ft)
                if math.isfinite(mt) and mt < best_try_m:
                    best_try_m = mt
                    if best_try is None:
                        best_try = ft.copy()
                    else:
                        best_try[:] = ft

            if best_try is not None:
                if best_try_m < mbest:
                    mbest = best_try_m
                    best = best_try.copy()
                    cur = best_try.copy()
                    mcur = best_try_m
                    fail = 0
                    improved_now = True
                elif best_try_m < mcur:
                    cur = best_try.copy()
                    mcur = best_try_m
                    fail = 0

        if not improved_now:
            fail += 1
            if fail == 2 or fail % 4 == 0:
                base = best if mbest < mcur else cur
                cur = _smooth_noise(base, rng, 0.12)
                mcur = _score(cur)
                if mcur < mbest:
                    mbest = mcur
                    best = cur.copy()
            elif fail % 2 == 0:
                cur = _smooth_noise(cur, rng, 0.04)
                mcur = _score(cur)
                if mcur < mbest:
                    mbest = mcur
                    best = cur.copy()

        # A few cheap random block perturbations from the current best.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline:
            for _ in range(6):
                cand = _random_block(best, rng)
                mc = _score(cand)
                if mc < mbest:
                    mbest = mc
                    best = cand.copy()
                    cur = best.copy()
                    mcur = mbest
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break

    return best, mbest, cur, mcur


def _polish(a, deadline, rng, top=180, ridx=64):
    n = int(a.size)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if n < 32 or S <= 0:
        return a, _score(a)

    a = _normalize(a, S)
    f0 = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    u = np.log(f0 / S)
    cur = a.copy()
    mcur = _score(cur)
    best = a.copy()
    mbest = mcur
    tau = max(0.05, 0.05 * max(mbest, 1.0))

    step = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and step < 5000:
        step += 1
        a = _normalize(np.exp(u - np.max(u)) * S, S)
        c = _conv_full(a)
        total = int(c.size)
        if total == 0:
            break

        ttop = min(top, total)
        trand = min(max(8, total // 500), ridx)
        top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -ttop)[-ttop:].astype(np.int64)
        rand_idx = rng.integers(0, total, trand).astype(np.int64)
        idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top_idx, rand_idx)))

        vals = c[idx]
        z = (vals - float(vals.max())) / tau
        w = np.exp(z)
        w /= w.sum()

        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk, ww in zip(idx, w):
            lo = max(0, int(kk) - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, int(kk))
            if lo <= hi:
                v[lo:hi + 1] += ww * a[int(kk) - np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)]

        gp = 2.0 * v
        gp -= gp.mean()
        scale = float(np.linalg.norm(gp))
        if scale < 1e-14:
            break

        lr = 0.10 / (scale / float(n) + 1e-14)
        accepted = False
        for mu in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05):
            u_try = u - lr * mu * gp
            a_try = _normalize(np.exp(u_try - np.max(u_try)) * S, S)
            mt = _score(a_try)
            if math.isfinite(mt) and mt < mcur:
                u = u_try.copy()
                cur = a_try.copy()
                mcur = mt
                if mt < mbest:
                    mbest = mt
                    best = cur.copy()
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            if step % 70 == 0:
                kick = rng.standard_normal(n)
                kick -= kick.mean()
                u = u + 0.02 * (float(np.linalg.norm(u)) / n + 1e-9) * kick
                cur = _normalize(np.exp(u - np.max(u)) * S, S)
                mcur = _score(cur)
                if mcur < mbest:
                    mbest = mcur
                    best = cur.copy()
            if tau > 0.012:
                tau *= 0.85

    return best, mbest


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    headroom = min(5.0, max(0.1, 0.02 * budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(0.1, budget_s - headroom)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = _parent_array()
    if parent.size >= 256:
        N = int(parent.size)
        init = parent.copy()
    else:
        N = 2048
        init = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)

    best = _normalize(init, math.sqrt(2.0 * N))
    best_score = _score(best)

    quick = np.ones(min(N, 64), dtype=np.float64)
    qs = _score(quick)
    if qs < best_score:
        best = quick.copy()
        best_score = qs

    def maybe_update(arr, sc):
        nonlocal best, best_score
        if arr is None:
            return
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return
        if not math.isfinite(sc) or sc >= best_score:
            return
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
        if arr.sum() <= 0:
            return
        best = arr.copy()
        best_score = float(sc)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
        coarse_n = None
        large_init = init.copy()

        if N > 3000:
            coarse_n = 1024
            coarse_end = min(deadline, t0 + 0.24 * budget_s)
        else:
            coarse_end = None

        coarse_best = None
        if coarse_n is not None and time.monotonic() < coarse_end - 30:
            cinit = _resample(init, coarse_n)
            if np.var(cinit) < 1e-18 * float(np.mean(cinit) ** 2):
                cinit = _smooth_noise(cinit, rng, 0.25)
            cb, cms, _, _ = _sota_loop(
                coarse_n,
                coarse_end,
                cinit,
                full=True,
                K_limit=2047,
                rng=rng,
                max_iter=45,
            )
            if cms < best_score:
                best = _normalize(cb, math.sqrt(2.0 * coarse_n)).copy()
                best_score = float(cms)
            if cms < 2.05:
                coarse_best = cb

        large_end = min(deadline, t0 + (0.66 * budget_s if N > 3000 else 0.78 * budget_s))
        if coarse_best is not None:
            large_init = _resample(coarse_best, N)
        else:
            large_init = _resample(init, N)

        if np.var(large_init) < 1e-18 * float(np.mean(large_init) ** 2):
            large_init = _smooth_noise(large_init, rng, 0.30)

        lb, lm, _, _ = _sota_loop(
            N,
            large_end,
            large_init,
            full=(N <= 1200),
            K_limit=800 if N > 4000 else 1200,
            rng=rng,
            max_iter=70,
        )
        maybe_update(lb, lm)

    # Polish the best current candidate until close to the deadline.
    polish_end = min(deadline, t0 + 0.95 * budget_s)
    if time.monotonic() < polish_end - 30:
        pa, ps = _polish(best, polish_end, rng)
        maybe_update(pa, ps)

    # Final cheap exploration from the best point.
    attempts = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.25:
        attempts += 1
        if attempts > 8000:
            break
        cand = _random_block(best, rng)
        sc = _score(cand)
        if sc < best_score:
            best = cand.copy()
            best_score = sc

    # Final validation against the authoritative evaluator.
    out = [float(x) for x in np.clip(best, 0.0, 1000.0)]
    out = [x for x in out if math.isfinite(x)]
    if len(out) == 0:
        out = [1.0]

    try:
        final_sc = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        if not math.isfinite(final_sc):
            out = [1.0]
    except Exception:
        out = [1.0]

    return out
```
